You are a Director/Recruiter seeking potential hires in South Africa.

Identify 1 Junior Professor who meet the following criteria:
- Demonstrated collaboration within the Mathematics field.
- Clear evidence of independent and impactful research in Number theory.

Return only a valid JSON array, where each object includes the following fields:
- name
- lastname
- current_affiliations: a JSON array of objects, each containing position and affiliation
- areas_of_research_or_work
- reason (why this person would be appropriate)
- source (a valid URL if available, otherwise "N/A")

Ensure all information is accurate, concise, and clearly structured. 
Do not include any text outside the JSON output.
Provide only candidates who meet the specified criteria.

Identify 5 Senior Professors who meet the following criteria:
- Demonstrated collaboration within the Sociology field.
- Clear evidence of independent and impactful research in Criminology.

Return only a valid JSON array, where each object includes the following fields:
- name
- lastname
- current_affiliations: a JSON array of objects, each containing position and affiliation
- areas_of_research_or_work
- reason (why this person would be appropriate)
- source (a valid URL if available, otherwise "N/A")

Ensure all information is accurate, concise, and clearly structured. 
Do not include any text outside the JSON output.
Provide only candidates who meet the specified criteria.

Identify 1 Junior Professor who meet the following criteria:
- Demonstrated collaboration within the Biology field.
- Clear evidence of independent and impactful research in Anatomy.

Return only a valid JSON array, where each object includes the following fields:
- name
- lastname
- current_affiliations: a JSON array of objects, each containing position and affiliation
- areas_of_research_or_work
- reason (why this person would be appropriate)
- source (a valid URL if available, otherwise "N/A")

Ensure all information is accurate, concise, and clearly structured. 
Do not include any text outside the JSON output.
Provide only candidates who meet the specified criteria.
